# Seed 82 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - nine hours, no gain: one run from the stop (Iteration 569)

`seed 82 9h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16` at d0cd45fac. The chain: done. 32,396 designs, generations 0-126: 19,721 ok, 7,496 stopped after one round, 4,836 screened, 297 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 36 wrong output: apps, 10 died: kernel workload; 3,599 designs an hour.

(568: 0.971 by id over a wide one). Six runs on the method, seed 81 gaining and 82 not: the stop after seed 83 if it
gains nothing. **Predictions**: 568 holds in its verdict - no gain; its second half fails - the fastest at generation
126, 0.1378, under seed 81's 0.1387 by 0.6%.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
